mwp_fw_discard_effect = {
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_dispatch_a
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_dispatch_b
remove_variable = mwp_fw_lane_b
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_regions
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_0
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_1
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_2
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_3
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_4
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_5
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_6
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_7
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_8
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_9
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_10
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_11
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_12
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_13
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_14
clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_queue_15
remove_variable = mwp_fw_stage
remove_variable = mwp_fw_epoch
remove_variable = mwp_fw_ticket
remove_variable = mwp_fw_bank
remove_variable = mwp_fw_length
remove_variable = mwp_fw_per_bank
remove_variable = mwp_fw_last_bank
remove_variable = mwp_fw_used
remove_variable = mwp_fw_strength
remove_variable = mwp_fw_region_count
}

mwp_fw_store_target_effect = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 7
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 3
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 1
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 0
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_0 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_1 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 2
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_2 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_3 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 5
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 4
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_4 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_5 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 6
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_6 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_7 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}

}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 11
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 9
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 8
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_8 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_9 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 10
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_10 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_11 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 13
}
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 12
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_12 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_13 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}
else = {
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_bank <= 14
}
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_14 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}
else = {
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_queue_15 target = scope:mwp_fw_target }
}

}

}

}

change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used add = 1 }
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_used >= var:mwp_fw_per_bank
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used value = 0 }
change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank add = 1 }
}

}

mwp_fw_index_rulers_effect = {
every_ruler = { limit = { is_alive = yes this != scope:mwp_player } add_to_list = mwp_fw_census }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_length value = list_size:mwp_fw_census }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_per_bank value = { value = var:mwp_fw_length divide = 16 ceiling = yes min = 48 } }
every_in_list = {
list = mwp_fw_census
save_scope_as = mwp_fw_target
scope:mwp_player = {
mwp_fw_store_target_effect = yes
}

}

set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank value = var:mwp_fw_bank }
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_used = 0 var:mwp_fw_last_bank > 0
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank subtract = 1 }
}


set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
}

mwp_fw_index_rivals_effect = {

set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_length value = var:mwp_war_scoring_rival_count }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_per_bank value = { value = var:mwp_fw_length divide = 16 ceiling = yes min = 48 } }
if = {
limit = {
has_variable_list = mwp_war_scoring_rivals
}
every_in_list = {
variable = mwp_war_scoring_rivals
save_scope_as = mwp_fw_target
scope:mwp_player = {
mwp_fw_store_target_effect = yes
}

}

}

set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank value = var:mwp_fw_bank }
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_used = 0 var:mwp_fw_last_bank > 0
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank subtract = 1 }
}


set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
}

mwp_fw_index_offensive_effect = {
save_scope_as = mwp_player
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_rank_cursor
    value = 0
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_high_count
    value = {
        value = var:mwp_war_offensive_count
        multiply = 0.1
        ceiling = yes
    }
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_through_medium_count
    value = {
        value = var:mwp_war_offensive_count
        multiply = 0.4
        ceiling = yes
    }
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_medium_count
    value = {
        value = var:mwp_war_offensive_through_medium_count
        subtract = var:mwp_war_offensive_high_count
    }
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_low_count
    value = {
        value = var:mwp_war_offensive_count
        subtract = var:mwp_war_offensive_through_medium_count
    }
}
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_valid_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_high_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_high_valid_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_medium_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_medium_valid_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_low_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_offensive_low_valid_sorted
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_length value = var:mwp_war_offensive_count }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_per_bank value = { value = var:mwp_fw_length divide = 16 ceiling = yes min = 48 } }
if = {
limit = {
has_variable_list = mwp_war_offensive_rivals
}
ordered_in_list = {
variable = mwp_war_offensive_rivals
order_by = var:mwp_war_offensive_sort_key
max = var:mwp_war_offensive_count
check_range_bounds = no
save_scope_as = mwp_fw_target
scope:mwp_player = {
mwp_fw_store_target_effect = yes
}

}

}

set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank value = var:mwp_fw_bank }
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_used = 0 var:mwp_fw_last_bank > 0
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank subtract = 1 }
}


set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
}

mwp_fw_rank_offensive_effect = {
save_scope_as = mwp_war_rival
scope:mwp_player = {
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_offensive_rank_cursor
        add = 1
    }
    add_to_variable_list = {
        name = mwp_info_offensive_sorted
        target = scope:mwp_war_rival
    }
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_rank
    value = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_offensive_rank_cursor
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_band_rank
    value = var:mwp_war_offensive_rank
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_offensive_likelihood
    value = 1
}
if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_rank <= scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_offensive_high_count
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_offensive_likelihood
        value = 3
    }
}
else_if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_rank <= scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_offensive_through_medium_count
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_offensive_likelihood
        value = 2
    }
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_offensive_band_rank
        subtract = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_offensive_high_count
    }
}
else = {
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_offensive_band_rank
        subtract = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_offensive_through_medium_count
    }
}
if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_likelihood = 3
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_likelihood_weight
        value = 16
    }
}
else_if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_likelihood = 2
        var:mwp_war_likelihood_weight < 4
    }
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_likelihood_weight
        value = 4
    }
}
if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_likelihood = 3
    }
    scope:mwp_player = {
        add_to_variable_list = {
            name = mwp_info_offensive_high_sorted
            target = scope:mwp_war_rival
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_war_offensive_available = 1
        }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = mwp_info_offensive_high_valid_sorted
                target = scope:mwp_war_rival
            }
        }
    }
}
else_if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_likelihood = 2
    }
    scope:mwp_player = {
        add_to_variable_list = {
            name = mwp_info_offensive_medium_sorted
            target = scope:mwp_war_rival
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_war_offensive_available = 1
        }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = mwp_info_offensive_medium_valid_sorted
                target = scope:mwp_war_rival
            }
        }
    }
}
else_if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_likelihood = 1
    }
    scope:mwp_player = {
        add_to_variable_list = {
            name = mwp_info_offensive_low_sorted
            target = scope:mwp_war_rival
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            var:mwp_war_offensive_available = 1
        }
        scope:mwp_player = {
            add_to_variable_list = {
                name = mwp_info_offensive_low_valid_sorted
                target = scope:mwp_war_rival
            }
        }
    }
}
if = {
    limit = {
        var:mwp_war_offensive_available = 1
    }
    scope:mwp_player = {
        add_to_variable_list = {
            name = mwp_info_offensive_valid_sorted
            target = scope:mwp_war_rival
        }
    }
}
}

mwp_fw_index_defensive_effect = {
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_defensive_rank_cursor
    value = 0
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_defensive_high_count
    value = {
        value = var:mwp_war_defensive_count
        multiply = 0.1
        ceiling = yes
    }
}

        multiply = 0.4
        ceiling = yes
    }
}

    value = {
        value = var:mwp_war_defensive_through_medium_count
        subtract = var:mwp_war_defensive_high_count
    }
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_defensive_low_count
    value = {
        value = var:mwp_war_defensive_count
        subtract = var:mwp_war_defensive_through_medium_count
    }
}

clear_variable_list = mwp_info_defensive_valid_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_defensive_high_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_defensive_high_valid_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_defensive_medium_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_defensive_medium_valid_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_defensive_low_sorted
clear_variable_list = mwp_info_defensive_low_valid_sorted
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_length value = var:mwp_war_defensive_count }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_per_bank value = { value = var:mwp_fw_length divide = 16 ceiling = yes min = 48 } }
if = {
limit = {
has_variable_list = mwp_war_defensive_rivals
}

mwp_fw_store_target_effect = yes
}

}

}

limit = {
var:mwp_fw_used = 0 var:mwp_fw_last_bank > 0
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank subtract = 1 }
}


set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }

mwp_fw_rank_defensive_effect = {
save_scope_as = mwp_war_rival
scope:mwp_player = {
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_defensive_rank_cursor
        add = 1
    }
    add_to_variable_list = {
        name = mwp_info_defensive_sorted
        target = scope:mwp_war_rival
    }
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_defensive_rank
    value = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_defensive_rank_cursor
}
set_variable = {
    name = mwp_war_defensive_band_rank
    value = var:mwp_war_defensive_rank
}

    name = mwp_war_defensive_likelihood
    value = 1
}

mwp_fw_scan_ruler_effect = {
save_scope_as = mwp_war_rival
scope:mwp_player = {
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_scanned_ruler_count
        add = 1
    }
}
if = {
    limit = {
        in_diplomatic_range = scope:mwp_player
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            scope:mwp_player = {
                OR = {
                    can_attack_in_hierarchy = scope:mwp_war_rival
                    has_cb_on = {
                        target = scope:mwp_war_rival
                        casus_belli = pam_humiliation_cb
                    }
                }
                has_any_cb_on = scope:mwp_war_rival
            }
        }
        mwp_scan_record_offensive_effect = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OR = {
                can_attack_in_hierarchy = scope:mwp_player
                has_cb_on = {
                    target = scope:mwp_player
                    casus_belli = pam_humiliation_cb
                }
            }
            has_any_cb_on = scope:mwp_player
        }
        mwp_scan_record_defensive_effect = yes
    }
}
}

mwp_fw_collect_geography_effect = {
save_scope_as = mwp_war_rival
if = {
limit = {
    exists = capital_province
}
capital_province = {
if = {
limit = {
exists = barony
}
barony = {
save_scope_as = mwp_fw_region scope:mwp_player = { add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_regions target = scope:mwp_fw_region } change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_region_count add = 1 } }
}

}
else = {
mwp_war_mark_region_capital_effect = yes
}

}

}
if = {
limit = {
    NOT = {
        any_liege_or_above = {
            has_variable = mwp_war_scoring_owner
            var:mwp_war_scoring_owner = scope:mwp_player
            has_variable = mwp_war_scoring_stamp
            var:mwp_war_scoring_stamp = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_scan_stamp
        }
    }
}
scope:mwp_player = {
    change_variable = {
        name = mwp_war_region_root_count
        add = 1
    }
}
every_sub_realm_county = {
save_scope_as = mwp_fw_region scope:mwp_player = { add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_regions target = scope:mwp_fw_region } change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_region_count add = 1 } }
}

}

}

mwp_fw_apply_geography_effect = {
if = {
limit = {
tier = tier_barony
}
title_province = {
mwp_war_mark_region_capital_effect = yes
}

}
else = {
if = {
    limit = {
        NOT = {
            AND = {
                exists = holder
                holder = {
                    OR = {
                        this = scope:mwp_player
                        is_vassal_or_below_of = scope:mwp_player
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    save_scope_value_as = {
        name = mwp_war_region_weight
        value = 1
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            exists = holder
        }
        holder = {
            mwp_war_region_holder_weight_effect = yes
        }
    }
    save_scope_value_as = {
        name = mwp_war_county_weight
        value = scope:mwp_war_region_weight
    }
    every_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
        limit = { tier = tier_barony }
        title_province ?= {
            scope:mwp_player = {
                change_variable = {
                    name = mwp_war_region_barony_visits
                    add = 1
                }
            }
            mwp_war_record_county_region_effect = yes
        }
    }
}
else = {
    every_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
        limit = { tier = tier_barony }
        title_province ?= {
            scope:mwp_player = {
                change_variable = {
                    name = mwp_war_region_barony_visits
                    add = 1
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
}

}

mwp_fw_index_geography_effect = {

set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_length value = var:mwp_fw_region_count }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_per_bank value = { value = var:mwp_fw_length divide = 16 ceiling = yes min = 48 } }
if = {
limit = {
has_variable_list = mwp_fw_regions
}
every_in_list = {
variable = mwp_fw_regions
save_scope_as = mwp_fw_target
scope:mwp_player = {
mwp_fw_store_target_effect = yes
}

}

}

set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank value = var:mwp_fw_bank }
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_used = 0 var:mwp_fw_last_bank > 0
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank subtract = 1 }
}

clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_regions
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
}

mwp_fw_index_borders_effect = {
every_sub_realm_county = { add_to_list = mwp_fw_border_counties }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_length value = list_size:mwp_fw_border_counties }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_used value = 0 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_per_bank value = { value = var:mwp_fw_length divide = 16 ceiling = yes min = 48 } }
every_in_list = {
list = mwp_fw_border_counties
save_scope_as = mwp_fw_target
scope:mwp_player = {
mwp_fw_store_target_effect = yes
}

}

set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank value = var:mwp_fw_bank }
if = {
limit = {
var:mwp_fw_used = 0 var:mwp_fw_last_bank > 0
}
change_variable = { name = mwp_fw_last_bank subtract = 1 }
}


set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_bank value = 0 }
}

mwp_fw_apply_border_effect = {
every_direct_de_jure_vassal_title = {
    limit = { tier = tier_barony }
    title_province ?= {
        every_neighboring_province = {
            limit = {
                exists = county
                NOT = {
                    mwp_war_province_in_player_subrealm_trigger = yes
                }
                county = {
                    exists = holder
                    holder = {
                        OR = {
                            AND = {
                                has_variable = mwp_war_scoring_owner
                                var:mwp_war_scoring_owner = scope:mwp_player
                                has_variable = mwp_war_scoring_stamp
                                var:mwp_war_scoring_stamp = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_scan_stamp
                            }
                            any_liege_or_above = {
                                has_variable = mwp_war_scoring_owner
                                var:mwp_war_scoring_owner = scope:mwp_player
                                has_variable = mwp_war_scoring_stamp
                                var:mwp_war_scoring_stamp = scope:mwp_player.var:mwp_war_scan_stamp
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            mwp_war_mark_region_border_effect = yes
        }
    }
}
}

mwp_fw_scan_composition_effect = {
save_scope_as = mwp_ec_primary
scope:mwp_player = {
    set_variable = {
        name = mwp_ec_rival
        value = scope:mwp_ec_primary
    }
    mwp_enemy_cache_prepare_scenario_effect = yes
}
}

mwp_fw_start_effect = {
 set_variable = { name = mwp_fp_init_generation value = var:mwp_generation }
 set_variable = { name = mwp_fp_init_cursor value = 0 }
mwp_fw_discard_effect = yes
save_scope_as = mwp_player
mwp_war_reset_effect = yes
mwp_read_analysis_strength_effect = {
    OUT = mwp_scan_player_strength
}
every_war_enemy = {
    limit = {
        is_alive = yes
        this != scope:mwp_player
    }
    save_scope_as = mwp_war_rival
    mwp_war_record_active_effect = yes
}
every_character_war = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            is_attacker = scope:mwp_player
        }
        primary_defender = {
            save_scope_as = mwp_war_rival
            mwp_war_record_scoring_union_effect = yes
        }
    }
    else = {
        primary_attacker = {
            save_scope_as = mwp_war_rival
            mwp_war_record_scoring_union_effect = yes
        }
    }
}
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_strength value = scope:mwp_scan_player_strength }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_epoch value = var:mwp_generation }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_stage value = 1 }
set_variable = { name = mwp_fw_ticket value = 1 }
add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_dispatch_a target = var:mwp_fw_ticket }
mwp_fw_index_rulers_effect = yes
}

mwp_fw_dispatch_effect = {
 if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_fp_init_cursor var:mwp_fp_init_cursor < 4 }
  if = { limit = { var:mwp_fp_init_cursor = 0 } mwp_fp_init_0_effect = yes }
else_if = { limit = { var:mwp_fp_init_cursor = 1 } mwp_fp_init_1_effect = yes }
else_if = { limit = { var:mwp_fp_init_cursor = 2 } mwp_fp_init_2_effect = yes }
else_if = { limit = { var:mwp_fp_init_cursor = 3 } mwp_fp_init_3_effect = yes }
  change_variable = { name = mwp_fp_init_cursor add = 1 }
 }
 mwp_fw_step_effect = yes
 if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_fw_stage } mwp_fw_step_effect = yes }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_fw_stage OR = { var:mwp_fw_stage = 1 var:mwp_fw_stage = 2 var:mwp_fw_stage = 3 var:mwp_fw_stage = 4 var:mwp_fw_stage = 5 var:mwp_fw_stage = 6 var:mwp_fw_stage = 7 var:mwp_fw_stage = 8 var:mwp_fw_stage = 10 var:mwp_fw_stage = 11 var:mwp_fw_stage = 12 var:mwp_fw_stage = 13 var:mwp_fw_stage = 14 var:mwp_fw_stage = 15 } } mwp_fw_step_effect = yes }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_fw_stage }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_fw_lane_b } clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_dispatch_b remove_variable = mwp_fw_lane_b add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_dispatch_a target = var:mwp_fw_ticket } }
 else = { clear_variable_list = mwp_fw_dispatch_a set_variable = mwp_fw_lane_b add_to_variable_list = { name = mwp_fw_dispatch_b target = var:mwp_fw_ticket } }
 }
}

mwp_fp_discard_effect = {
 clear_variable_list = mwp_fp_a
 clear_variable_list = mwp_fp_b
 remove_variable = mwp_fp_step
 remove_variable = mwp_fp_generation
}

mwp_fp_cancel_effect = {
 if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_fa_profile }
        mwp_fa_restore_combat_effect = yes
    }
    if = { limit = { has_variable = mwp_fa_live } mwp_fa_discard_effect = yes }
 mwp_fp_discard_effect = yes
}
